每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38810821322 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 5

重要指引缺失上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · references/testing.md 未说明 cookie 写请求需要 trustedOrigins 与 Origin 头

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

testing.md 的整应用测试小节只给出 GET 示例，全文无 Origin/trustedOrigins 说明（grep 无命中）；而 auth.js 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 对带 cookie 的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求在缺少可信 origin 时返回 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN，探针日志 E59 在同一 helper 上实测 POST/PATCH 403、GET 200。应用只能自配 trustedOrigins 并手动补 Origin 头（E34/E60）。属文档 guidance-gap，非运行时缺陷主张。

  • 职责与公开约定：testing.md 承诺用 createAppTest + signIn 走真实登录测试整应用路由鉴权，示例只发 GET，未说明写请求所需的 Origin/trustedOrigins；helper 文档语义是'以已登录用户身份请求'。 E24 E22
  • 实际行为证据：auth.js 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 对 cookie 认证的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求在 source 缺失或不信任时返回 invalidCsrfOrigin；E59 记录同一会话 POST/PATCH 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN、GET 200。 E23 E59
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用未误用 API，而是显式配置 config.auth.trustedOrigins 并在写请求补 Origin 头（E34），且把该要求写进 server/AGENTS.md（E60），说明现有文档未覆盖。 E34 E60
  • 环境因素：现象在 createTestApp 启动的真实应用与测试数据库上复现（E21/E59），非外部服务、凭据或依赖缺失所致；403 由本应用认证中间件返回。 E21 E59
  • 工厂因素：该文档属模板自带的 nocobase-app-development Skill（E24），未见工厂提示或 QA/构建脚本改写其内容；无工厂材料可解释该缺口。 E24
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开路径只有 testing.md 的整应用测试指引与 signIn helper，二者都不发送 Origin、也未提供写请求配置；cli.md 的 origin 指引针对部署 publicOrigin，不覆盖测试 CSRF。 E24 E22 E66

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

testing.md 的整应用测试示例只发起 GET，未提及写请求；而 authentication 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 会对 cookie 认证的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求校验 origin/referer 是否可信，缺失即返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN。Agent 按文档写出 POST 测试后会先失败。

实际影响
Agent 需要反复试错并回读 authentication 源码才能让写接口测试通过，增加接入时间与错误率。
建议改法
在 testing.md 的示例后补充：写请求需在 createAppTest 的 config.auth 中设置 trustedOrigins 并携带匹配的 Origin 头（或说明 TestSession.fetch 的行为）。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
按 testing.md 示例编写 cookie 认证的 POST/PATCH 集成测试
应有能力 / 行为
文档给出可完成写请求的最小配置（trustedOrigins + Origin）
实际观察
文档仅展示 GET；写请求返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN，需从 auth.js 源码推断
应用绕行与代价
应用中配置 config.auth.trustedOrigins 并为写请求手动加 Origin 头
改进后的验收标准
testing.md 含一个通过写请求的示例，照抄即可 200
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–73E24

testing.md documents createAppTest({createServer: createStandaloneServer}) and signIn(...) but the example only issues a GET and never mentions Origin/trustedOrigins.

## Testing through the whole application

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';
import { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';
import { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';

const test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });

test('lists orders for an administrator only', async ({ testApp, request }) => {
  expect((await request('/orders')).status).toBe(401);
  const admin = await signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);
  expect((await admin.fetch('/orders')).status).toBe(200);
});
```

The application starts as `pnpm start` starts it and installs its migrations and seeds on start; one application serves the test file. A user other than the administrator is one a seed of the application created, signed in the same way.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–49E22

signIn posts to the app's own sign-in route and TestSession.fetch only sets the cookie header; TEST_ORIGIN is defined but never applied to requests.

export async function signIn(app, credentials) {
    const apiRoot = `${TEST_ORIGIN}${trimTrailingSlash(app.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const byUsername = 'username' in credentials;
    const response = await app.fetch(new Request(`${apiRoot}/auth/sign-in/${byUsername ? 'username' : 'email'}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    }));
    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Sign-in as "${byUsername ? credentials.username : credentials.email}" failed with ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
    }
    const body = (await response.json());
    if (!body.user) {
        throw new Error('Sign-in succeeded without returning the signed-in user.');
    }
    const cookie = response.headers
        .getSetCookie()
        .map((header) => header.split(';')[0])
        .join('; ');
    return {
        user: body.user,
        cookie,
        fetch: async (path, init = {}) => {
            const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
            headers.set('cookie', [headers.get('cookie'), cookie].filter(Boolean).join('; '));
            return app.fetch(new Request(path.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${path}` : path, {
                ...init,
                headers,
            }));
        },
    };
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:214–250E23

checkCookieWriteOrigin answers invalidCsrfOrigin for cookie-authenticated non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS requests whose origin/referer is missing or untrusted.

    /** Protect writes authenticated by a browser cookie, including routes that skip normal session lookup. */
    async checkCookieWriteOrigin(context) {
        if (['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(context.req.method))
            return;
        // A credential header is not proof that it was used: an invalid API key may fall back to a valid cookie.
        // Cookie-free API key requests have no ambient browser credential to forge.
        if (!context.req.header('cookie'))
            return;
        const authContext = await this.auth.$context;
        const origin = context.req.header('origin');
        const inferredBaseURL = origin === 'null' &&
            context.req.header('sec-fetch-site') === 'same-origin'
            ? getBaseURL(undefined, authContext.options.basePath, context.req.raw, false, authContext.options.advanced?.trustedProxyHeaders)
            : undefined;
        const source = (inferredBaseURL ? getOrigin(inferredBaseURL) : undefined) ??
            origin ??
            context.req.header('referer');
        if (!source || source === 'null') {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
        // Better Auth also accepts per-request trusted origins. Its static context contains the
        // configured base URL, static origins, plugin origins, and BETTER_AUTH_TRUSTED_ORIGINS.
        const configuredOrigins = authContext.options.trustedOrigins;
        const mergedOrigins = typeof configuredOrigins === 'function'
            ? await configuredOrigins(context.req.raw)
            : (configuredOrigins ?? []);
        const requestAuthContext = Object.create(authContext);
        requestAuthContext.trustedOrigins = [
            ...authContext.trustedOrigins,
            ...mergedOrigins.filter((origin) => typeof origin === 'string' && Boolean(origin)),
        ];
        const trustedByAuth = requestAuthContext.isTrustedOrigin(source, {
            allowRelativePaths: false,
        });
        if (!trustedByAuth) {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }

app/tests/logic/todos-api.test.ts:11–29E34

The app's test workaround passes createStandaloneServer, configures auth.secret and auth.trustedOrigins, and adds an Origin header to writes.

const test = createAppTest({
  createServer: createStandaloneServer,
  config: {
    auth: {
      secret=[REDACTED]',
      // A browser writes with a cookie and sends its own origin; the test's in-process requests have to say which
      // origin they claim to be, and the application has to trust it, exactly as a deployment trusts its own.
      trustedOrigins: ['http://localhost'],
    },
  },
});

const TEST_ORIGIN = 'http://localhost';

const postTodo = (session: TestSession, body: unknown) =>
  session.fetch('/todos', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', origin: TEST_ORIGIN },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [重要][指引缺失] references/testing.md 未说明 cookie 写请求需要 trustedOrigins 与 Origin 头

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.4）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

testing.md 的整应用测试小节只给出 GET 示例，全文无 Origin/trustedOrigins 说明（grep 无命中）；而 auth.js 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 对带 cookie 的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求在缺少可信 origin 时返回 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN，探针日志 E59 在同一 helper 上实测 POST/PATCH 403、GET 200。应用只能自配 trustedOrigins 并手动补 Origin 头（E34/E60）。属文档 guidance-gap，非运行时缺陷主张。

- 职责与公开约定：testing.md 承诺用 createAppTest + signIn 走真实登录测试整应用路由鉴权，示例只发 GET，未说明写请求所需的 Origin/trustedOrigins；helper 文档语义是'以已登录用户身份请求'。；证据：E24（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–73）、E22（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–49）
- 实际行为证据：auth.js 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 对 cookie 认证的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求在 source 缺失或不信任时返回 invalidCsrfOrigin；E59 记录同一会话 POST/PATCH 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN、GET 200。；证据：E23（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:214–250）、E59（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0035.txt:1591–1594）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用未误用 API，而是显式配置 config.auth.trustedOrigins 并在写请求补 Origin 头（E34），且把该要求写进 server/AGENTS.md（E60），说明现有文档未覆盖。；证据：E34（app/tests/logic/todos-api.test.ts:11–29）、E60（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0053.txt:1506–1507）
- 环境因素：现象在 createTestApp 启动的真实应用与测试数据库上复现（E21/E59），非外部服务、凭据或依赖缺失所致；403 由本应用认证中间件返回。；证据：E21（packages/@nocobase/app-testing/dist/src/server/app.js:9–30）、E59（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0035.txt:1591–1594）
- 工厂因素：该文档属模板自带的 nocobase-app-development Skill（E24），未见工厂提示或 QA/构建脚本改写其内容；无工厂材料可解释该缺口。；证据：E24（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–73）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开路径只有 testing.md 的整应用测试指引与 signIn helper，二者都不发送 Origin、也未提供写请求配置；cli.md 的 origin 指引针对部署 publicOrigin，不覆盖测试 CSRF。；证据：E24（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–73）、E22（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–49）、E66（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/cli.md:43–43）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

testing.md 的整应用测试示例只发起 GET，未提及写请求；而 authentication 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 会对 cookie 认证的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求校验 origin/referer 是否可信，缺失即返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN。Agent 按文档写出 POST 测试后会先失败。

**影响**：Agent 需要反复试错并回读 authentication 源码才能让写接口测试通过，增加接入时间与错误率。

**建议改动**：在 testing.md 的示例后补充：写请求需在 createAppTest 的 config.auth 中设置 trustedOrigins 并携带匹配的 Origin 头（或说明 TestSession.fetch 的行为）。

**验收标准**：testing.md 含一个通过写请求的示例，照抄即可 200

- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–49` — signIn posts to the app's own sign-in route and TestSession.fetch only sets the cookie header; TEST_ORIGIN is defined but never applied to requests.
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:214–250` — checkCookieWriteOrigin answers invalidCsrfOrigin for cookie-authenticated non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS requests whose origin/referer is missing or untrusted.
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0035.txt:1591–1594` — Probe log: cookie-authenticated POST/PATCH answer 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN while GET succeeds.
- `app/tests/logic/todos-api.test.ts:11–29` — The app's test workaround passes createStandaloneServer, configures auth.secret and auth.trustedOrigins, and adds an Origin header to writes.

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38810821322 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
轻微指引错误上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F3 · nocobase-db Skill 的 seed 示例与该仓库基线 ESLint 规则冲突

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/db · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

nocobase-db SKILL.md 的 seed 示例解构 run({ repository }) 后调用 repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne（E12），而应用基线 lint 通过 @nocobase/dev-config/eslint 的 recommendedTypeChecked 启用 @typescript-eslint/unbound-method（E63），E58 记录实际报错于该 seed 写法；应用改用 run(context) + context.repository('todos')（E31）才通过。属 guidance-gap。

  • 职责与公开约定：SKILL.md 的 seed/migration 示例是要求照抄的规范代码；示例用 run({ repository }) 解构并直接调用 repository(...)，文档未提示该写法与 lint 的冲突（E12）；默认 lint 链启用 unbound-method（E63）。 E12 E63
  • 实际行为证据：默认 typescript 配置展开 recommendedTypeChecked，包含 unbound-method（E63）；E58 记录 eslint 对该 seed 模式报 unbound-method，属实际运行观察。 E63 E58
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用的可用 seed 用 run(context) 并把 repository 作为 context 方法调用（E31），即必须偏离 Skill 示例才能通过。 E31
  • 环境因素：该规则来自已安装的 @nocobase/dev-config/eslint（E63）并由 app/eslint.config.js 引入，E58 在同一环境触发；非环境偶发。 E63 E58
  • 工厂因素：工厂配置附加在应用配置之后，但 E58 证明实际 lint 确实报错，说明没有工厂覆盖消除该问题；冲突写法在 Skill 源文件 E12。 E58 E12
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：Skill 的 troubleshooting / 'API shapes that do not exist' 只纠正 db.repository() 缺名等，不涉及解构丢失 this 的 lint 冲突；现有指引无法让示例原样通过。 E13 E12

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

SKILL.md 的 seed 示例把 repository 解构为独立方法再调用，与该仓库默认 lint 规则冲突；Agent 照抄会在提交前被 ESLint 拦下。

评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

Skill 示例：async run({ repository }) { await repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne({...}) }

评审时 · 实际观察

同一解构写法触发默认 @typescript-eslint/unbound-method（数据库方法丢失 this），应用改为 async run(context) { context.repository('todos') } 后才通过 lint。

实际影响
增加一次无谓的写错-修正循环，并可能让 Agent 误以为 @nocobase/db 用法有误。
建议改法
示例改为 async run(context) { const repository = context.repository; ... } 或直接 context.repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne(...)，并在 troubleshooting 表补充该 lint 提示。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准

应有能力 / 行为
示例代码通过仓库基线 lint
实际观察
@typescript-eslint/unbound-method 报错，需改写调用方式
应用绕行与代价
改用 run(context) 并把 repository 作为对象方法调用

app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:116–131E12

The seed example destructures run({ repository }) and calls repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne(...).

```ts
// database/main/seeds/202609030002_default_order_statuses.ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609030002_default_order_statuses',

  async run({ repository }) {
    await repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne({
      filter: { code: 'draft' },
      create: { code: 'draft', title: 'Draft' },
      update: { title: 'Draft' },
    });
  },
});
```

    /** Collection schema and metadata builder. Uses Collection and Field logical names. */
    builder(name?: string): CollectionBuilder;

     */
    collections(name?: string): ConnectionCollections;

app/database/main/seeds/202610100002_seed_todos.ts:25–58E31

The required seed uses defineSeed, run(context) and context.repository<TodoRow>('todos'), and is idempotent by title.

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202610100002_seed_todos',

  async run(context) {
    const todos = context.repository<TodoRow>('todos');
    const now = Date.now();
    // `dueAt` is an RFC 3339 instant: a deadline two days in the past, one a week out, and one already done.
    const rows = [
      {
        title: 'Submit the quarterly report',
        dueAt: new Date(now - 2 * DAY_MS).toISOString(),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: 'Prepare the release notes',
        dueAt: new Date(now + 7 * DAY_MS).toISOString(),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: 'Review the API documentation',
        dueAt: new Date(now + DAY_MS).toISOString(),
        completed: true,
        expired: false,
      },
    ];

    for (const row of rows) {
      const existing = await todos.findOne({ filter: { title: row.title } });
      if (existing) continue;
      await todos.createOne({ values: row });
    }
  },

### [轻微][指引错误] nocobase-db Skill 的 seed 示例与该仓库基线 ESLint 规则冲突

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

nocobase-db SKILL.md 的 seed 示例解构 run({ repository }) 后调用 repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne（E12），而应用基线 lint 通过 @nocobase/dev-config/eslint 的 recommendedTypeChecked 启用 @typescript-eslint/unbound-method（E63），E58 记录实际报错于该 seed 写法；应用改用 run(context) + context.repository('todos')（E31）才通过。属 guidance-gap。

- 职责与公开约定：SKILL.md 的 seed/migration 示例是要求照抄的规范代码；示例用 run({ repository }) 解构并直接调用 repository(...)，文档未提示该写法与 lint 的冲突（E12）；默认 lint 链启用 unbound-method（E63）。；证据：E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:116–131）、E63（packages/@nocobase/dev-config/dist/eslint/index.js:78–80）
- 实际行为证据：默认 typescript 配置展开 recommendedTypeChecked，包含 unbound-method（E63）；E58 记录 eslint 对该 seed 模式报 unbound-method，属实际运行观察。；证据：E63（packages/@nocobase/dev-config/dist/eslint/index.js:78–80）、E58（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0061.txt:21–25）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用的可用 seed 用 run(context) 并把 repository 作为 context 方法调用（E31），即必须偏离 Skill 示例才能通过。；证据：E31（app/database/main/seeds/202610100002_seed_todos.ts:25–58）
- 环境因素：该规则来自已安装的 @nocobase/dev-config/eslint（E63）并由 app/eslint.config.js 引入，E58 在同一环境触发；非环境偶发。；证据：E63（packages/@nocobase/dev-config/dist/eslint/index.js:78–80）、E58（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0061.txt:21–25）
- 工厂因素：工厂配置附加在应用配置之后，但 E58 证明实际 lint 确实报错，说明没有工厂覆盖消除该问题；冲突写法在 Skill 源文件 E12。；证据：E58（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0061.txt:21–25）、E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:116–131）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：Skill 的 troubleshooting / 'API shapes that do not exist' 只纠正 db.repository() 缺名等，不涉及解构丢失 this 的 lint 冲突；现有指引无法让示例原样通过。；证据：E13（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:381–390）、E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:116–131）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

SKILL.md 的 seed 示例把 repository 解构为独立方法再调用，与该仓库默认 lint 规则冲突；Agent 照抄会在提交前被 ESLint 拦下。

**文档 / API 声称**：Skill 示例：async run({ repository }) { await repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne({...}) }

**实际观察**：同一解构写法触发默认 @typescript-eslint/unbound-method（数据库方法丢失 this），应用改为 async run(context) { context.repository('todos') } 后才通过 lint。

**影响**：增加一次无谓的写错-修正循环，并可能让 Agent 误以为 @nocobase/db 用法有误。

**建议改动**：示例改为 async run(context) { const repository = context.repository; ... } 或直接 context.repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne(...)，并在 troubleshooting 表补充该 lint 提示。

**验收标准**：Skill 示例原样通过 ESLint

F2 · signIn 的 TestSession.fetch 只带 cookie，不发送已定义的 TEST_ORIGIN

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

signIn 返回的 TestSession.fetch 只合并 cookie 头，TEST_ORIGIN 仅用于拼 apiRoot，从不作为请求头；服务端 checkCookieWriteOrigin 要求可信 source，因此该公开 helper 的写请求必然 403（E59 实测）。可改 helper 或补文档，属可用性改进。

  • 职责与公开约定：testing.js 公开 signIn/TestSession.fetch，文档把它作为以已登录用户身份发起请求的入口；契约包含'携带会话 cookie 调用 fetch'，但未声明写请求需额外头。 E22 E24
  • 实际行为证据：E22 显示 fetch 只写 cookie 头且 TEST_ORIGIN 未用于请求；E23 服务端在缺可信 origin 时拒绝；E59 实测 POST/PATCH 403、GET 200。 E22 E23 E59
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用通过调用方补 Origin 头并配置 trustedOrigins 绕行（E34/E60），未修改 helper；是 helper 能力边界而非应用误用。 E34 E60
  • 环境因素：在真实应用+测试数据库环境复现（E21/E59），排除外部服务、凭据或安装问题。 E21 E59
  • 工厂因素：helper 是插件包内公开源码（E22），无工厂提示或脚本介入；缺口源于 helper 自身实现。 E22
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：fetch 没有 origin 参数或默认头，文档也未说明限制或替代，现有公开能力不足以开箱完成写请求。 E22 E24

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

testing.js 中 TEST_ORIGIN='http://localhost' 已知且用于拼 apiRoot，但 TestSession.fetch 只设置 cookie 头，不设置 Origin。写请求因此无法满足 checkCookieWriteOrigin，除非应用另行信任并手动加头。

实际影响
公开的登录测试 helper 对写请求开箱不可用，违背 helper 的直觉用途。
建议改法
让 TestSession.fetch 默认发送 Origin: TEST_ORIGIN（或在文档中明确其不适用于写请求并给出替代）。

应有能力 / 行为

实际观察
fetch 不带 Origin，服务端 CSRF 校验拒绝
应用绕行与代价

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–49E22

signIn posts to the app's own sign-in route and TestSession.fetch only sets the cookie header; TEST_ORIGIN is defined but never applied to requests.

export async function signIn(app, credentials) {
    const apiRoot = `${TEST_ORIGIN}${trimTrailingSlash(app.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const byUsername = 'username' in credentials;
    const response = await app.fetch(new Request(`${apiRoot}/auth/sign-in/${byUsername ? 'username' : 'email'}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    }));
    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Sign-in as "${byUsername ? credentials.username : credentials.email}" failed with ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
    }
    const body = (await response.json());
    if (!body.user) {
        throw new Error('Sign-in succeeded without returning the signed-in user.');
    }
    const cookie = response.headers
        .getSetCookie()
        .map((header) => header.split(';')[0])
        .join('; ');
    return {
        user: body.user,
        cookie,
        fetch: async (path, init = {}) => {
            const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
            headers.set('cookie', [headers.get('cookie'), cookie].filter(Boolean).join('; '));
            return app.fetch(new Request(path.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${path}` : path, {
                ...init,
                headers,
            }));
        },
    };
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:214–250E23

checkCookieWriteOrigin answers invalidCsrfOrigin for cookie-authenticated non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS requests whose origin/referer is missing or untrusted.

    /** Protect writes authenticated by a browser cookie, including routes that skip normal session lookup. */
    async checkCookieWriteOrigin(context) {
        if (['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(context.req.method))
            return;
        // A credential header is not proof that it was used: an invalid API key may fall back to a valid cookie.
        // Cookie-free API key requests have no ambient browser credential to forge.
        if (!context.req.header('cookie'))
            return;
        const authContext = await this.auth.$context;
        const origin = context.req.header('origin');
        const inferredBaseURL = origin === 'null' &&
            context.req.header('sec-fetch-site') === 'same-origin'
            ? getBaseURL(undefined, authContext.options.basePath, context.req.raw, false, authContext.options.advanced?.trustedProxyHeaders)
            : undefined;
        const source = (inferredBaseURL ? getOrigin(inferredBaseURL) : undefined) ??
            origin ??
            context.req.header('referer');
        if (!source || source === 'null') {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
        // Better Auth also accepts per-request trusted origins. Its static context contains the
        // configured base URL, static origins, plugin origins, and BETTER_AUTH_TRUSTED_ORIGINS.
        const configuredOrigins = authContext.options.trustedOrigins;
        const mergedOrigins = typeof configuredOrigins === 'function'
            ? await configuredOrigins(context.req.raw)
            : (configuredOrigins ?? []);
        const requestAuthContext = Object.create(authContext);
        requestAuthContext.trustedOrigins = [
            ...authContext.trustedOrigins,
            ...mergedOrigins.filter((origin) => typeof origin === 'string' && Boolean(origin)),
        ];
        const trustedByAuth = requestAuthContext.isTrustedOrigin(source, {
            allowRelativePaths: false,
        });
        if (!trustedByAuth) {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }

评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:8–20E6

operations.md lists the management HTTP routes and states the plugin contributes no pages, so the application draws its own schedule list.

## Management Entry Points

The plugin contributes no pages: the application builds its schedule list and detail pages on the routes below and declares them among its own routes, guarded by the same permission. Definitions are maintained in source. The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions.

All routes below require authentication and authorization for `{ resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' }, action: 'access' }`, checked before the schedule is looked up. Enabling/disabling currently uses the same permission, not a separate update action.

| Method and path                                        | Response / effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules`                         | `{ data: ScheduleListItem[], meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`; `page` (default 1) and `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100)                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId`             | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences` | `{ data: ScheduleOccurrenceView[], meta: { nextPageToken? } }`, newest first; `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100) and `pageToken` from the previous page's `meta` |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable`     | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; enables the task, no request body                                                                                                    |

  {
    // The Scheduler plugin owns the schedules, their history and this page's permission, but contributes no page of
    // its own — so the application draws the list its administrators read. Stored page grants reference
    // `scheduler.schedules`, which is why the resource id is the plugin's and not a new one.
    auth: 'required',
    authz: {
      resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' },
      action: 'access',
    },
    componentLoader: () => import('./pages/scheduler/index.js'),
    name: 'scheduler',
    navigation: { title: 'navigation.scheduler', icon: CalendarClockIcon },
    path: '/scheduler',
  },

### [轻微][易用性改进] Scheduler 插件不提供管理页面，每个应用都要自建计划与运行记录页

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.16）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

operations.md 明确插件不贡献页面，只提供 HTTP 路由与 schedulerServiceToken（E6/E3）；插件 client 入口只注册 locales、注释亦声明 no pages（E64）。本应用为此自建 client/pages/scheduler/* 路由（E33），QA 报告 B01–B03 才通过（E35）。属可复用的插件能力改进建议。

- 职责与公开约定：operations.md 声明插件只提供管理路由且 'contributes no pages'，应用需自行声明页面（E6）；tokens.js 确认仅公开 schedulerServiceToken，不含任何页面/UI 入口（E3）。；证据：E6（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:8–20）、E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/tokens.js:1–8）
- 实际行为证据：E64 显示插件 client 仅 defineClientPlugin 注册 locales；E33 应用自建 /scheduler 路由；E35 QA 通过列表/启停/运行记录验收，证明页面确实是应用侧实现。；证据：E64（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/client/plugin.js:3–10）、E33（app/client/routes.ts:48–61）、E35（artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–22）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用按 operations.md 的路由与 scheduler.schedules 权限正确装配自建页面（E33），非误用 API。；证据：E33（app/client/routes.ts:48–61）
- 环境因素：插件与页面在运行应用上通过 B01–B03（E35），排除环境/依赖问题；无页面是插件设计事实。；证据：E35（artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–22）
- 工厂因素：operations.md 是插件自带 Skill 指引（E6），未见工厂提示或脚本改写该无页面约定。；证据：E6（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:8–20）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开能力仅 HTTP API 与 sync 命令、client 无页面（E64/E6），因此每个应用仍需重复实现管理视图；改进建议成立。；证据：E64（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/client/plugin.js:3–10）、E6（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:8–20）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

operations.md 明确插件不贡献页面，只有 HTTP API 与 sync 命令；本应用因此新增 client/pages/scheduler/* 才能满足 B01–B03 的人工验收路径。

**影响**：常见的管理需求需要在每个应用重复实现列表/启用停用/运行记录视图，拉长交付并引入不一致。

**建议改动**：提供一个可选的、位于 scheduler.schedules 权限下的管理页面（或作为模板示例），插件仍可保持无页面为默认。

**验收标准**：新应用无需自写页面即可完成列表/启停/查看运行记录

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:8–20` — operations.md lists the management HTTP routes and states the plugin contributes no pages, so the application draws its own schedule list.
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/tokens.js:1–8` — schedulerServiceToken is the plugin's single public service; store, target registry and occurrence history stay private.
- `app/client/routes.ts:48–61` — The application builds its own /scheduler page route with auth required and page grant scheduler.schedules, plus the /todos routes.
- `artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–22` — QA report passes B01/B02/B03: real cron runs with recorded results, idempotent re-run keeps 3 todos, enable/disable persists across refresh.

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38810821322 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
轻微易用性改进上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审：待确认（suspected）
F7 · @nocobase/app-server/package.json 不能通过 require 读取

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-server · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

@nocobase/app-server 的 exports 仅列出 ./application、.、./config 等子路径，未导出 './package.json'（E65）；E45 探针记录 require('@nocobase/app-server/package.json') 返回 ERR_PACKAGE_PATH_NOT_EXPORTED。属包元数据读取的可用性改进，非应用缺陷。

  • 职责与公开约定：package.json 的 exports 映射枚举了公开子路径且缺 './package.json'（E65）；routes.d.ts 显示该包以显式导出入口对外（E14），未提供元数据入口。 E65 E14
  • 实际行为证据：E45 是实际探查输出，require 裸子路径被 exports 拦截；E65 源码解释为没有 './package.json' 导出。 E45 E65
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用/Agent 只是读取包元数据受挫（E45），没有装配或调用错误。 E45
  • 环境因素：包已安装（basis 列 @nocobase/app-server 2.0.0-beta.3），失败来自 Node exports 解析而非缺依赖或网络（E45/E65）。 E45 E65
  • 工厂因素：该 exports 由包自身 package.json 决定（E65），无工厂提示/脚本注入因素。 E65
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有导出入口提供运行时 API，但没有任何入口返回包版本/元数据；调用方须改用 fs 路径等非裸规范方式，改进空间成立。 E65 E14

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

Agent 探查时常 require('<包>/package.json') 读取版本/入口，本包 exports 映射未导出 package.json，返回 ERR_PACKAGE_PATH_NOT_EXPORTED。

实际影响
Agent 需要改用其它方式获取包信息，属轻微摩擦；无法确认是否所有包都如此。
建议改法
若与其它包不一致，考虑在 exports 中导出 './package.json' 或在文档中给出读取包元数据的方式。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
require('@nocobase/app-server/package.json')
应有能力 / 行为
能读到包元数据
实际观察
ERR_PACKAGE_PATH_NOT_EXPORTED
应用绕行与代价
改用 import.meta 或直接读依赖目录，或使用包提供的公开入口
改进后的验收标准
有明确且文档化的方式读取包版本/入口

packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.d.ts:1–24E14

defineApiRoutes/defineRootRoutes return typed AppApiRouteContribution with scope 'api'/'root'; defineHttpMiddleware is the other entry point.

import type { Hono } from 'hono';
export type AppRouterFactory<TApplication> = (app: TApplication) => Hono | Promise<Hono>;
export type AppHttpMiddlewareRegister<TApplication> = (router: Hono, app: TApplication) => void | Promise<void>;
export interface AppHttpMiddleware<TApplication> {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly register: AppHttpMiddlewareRegister<TApplication>;
}
export interface AppApiRouteContribution<TApplication> {
    readonly scope: 'api';
    readonly createRouter: AppRouterFactory<TApplication>;
}
export interface AppRootRouteContribution<TApplication> {
    readonly scope: 'root';
    readonly createRouter: AppRouterFactory<TApplication>;
}
export type AppRouteContribution<TApplication> = AppApiRouteContribution<TApplication> | AppRootRouteContribution<TApplication>;
export interface DefineHttpMiddlewareOptions<TApplication> {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly register: AppHttpMiddlewareRegister<TApplication>;
}
export declare function defineHttpMiddleware<TApplication>(options: DefineHttpMiddlewareOptions<TApplication>): AppHttpMiddleware<TApplication>;
export declare function defineApiRoutes<TApplication>(createRouter: AppRouterFactory<TApplication>): AppApiRouteContribution<TApplication>;
export declare function defineRootRoutes<TApplication>(createRouter: AppRouterFactory<TApplication>): AppRootRouteContribution<TApplication>;
//# sourceMappingURL=routes.d.ts.map

Agent 探查时常 require('<包>/package.json') 读取版本/入口，本包 exports 映射未导出 package.json，返回 ERR_PACKAGE_PATH_NOT_EXPORTED。

**影响**：Agent 需要改用其它方式获取包信息，属轻微摩擦；无法确认是否所有包都如此。

**建议改动**：若与其它包不一致，考虑在 exports 中导出 './package.json' 或在文档中给出读取包元数据的方式。

**验收标准**：有明确且文档化的方式读取包版本/入口

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0023.txt:38–42` — Raw JSONL window around an indexed error signal: Exploration: require('@nocobase/app-server/package.json') is blocked by the exports map (ERR_PACKAGE_PATH_NOT_EXPORTED).
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/routes.d.ts:1–24` — defineApiRoutes/defineRootRoutes return typed AppApiRouteContribution with scope 'api'/'root'; defineHttpMiddleware is the other entry point.

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38810821322 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
实现者过程与运行背景 · 5 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

整体顺利：待办表/接口/页面/调度目标一次成型，主要时间花在把第一个 Scheduler 端到端测试跑通（createAppTest、signIn、cookie 写 origin）以及等待一次真实 cron 触发（约 61s/次）。

问题 01 · 实现阶段

用 createAppTest 写调度 E2E 时反复 404 / createServer is not a function

本任务已处理

不传 createServer 抛 TypeError: createServer is not a function；把 application 传给 signIn 或直接 fetch 时返回 404。

原因（实现者自述）
@nocobase/app-testing/server 必须由应用提供自己的 server 工厂；且只有 TestApp 的 fetch 会带上 APP_BASE_PATH，Application 自己的 fetch 不会。
处理方式
传 createServer: createStandaloneServer（来自 ../../server/standalone.js），并用 signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS) 返回的 cookie/fetch 发请求。
影响 / 代价：约 30 分钟，多跑 3 轮修复来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · 实现阶段

API 测试里的写请求被 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN 拒绝

本任务已处理

POST/PATCH 返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN，读请求正常。

原因（实现者自述）
Cookie 认证的写请求会校验 origin，而测试未配置 auth.trustedOrigins 也未发送 origin 头。
处理方式
测试应用配置 auth: { secret, trustedOrigins: ['http://localhost'] }，写请求带 origin: 'http://localhost'。
影响 / 代价：约 15 分钟来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 03 · 实现阶段

require seed 的 seed 测试报 Collection resolution failed: $.name

本任务已处理

database.repository<T>() 直接调用时抛 Collection name must be a non-empty string。

原因（实现者自述）
Repository 必须显式传集合名，泛型只声明行类型。
处理方式
改为 database.repository<T>('todos')，并把 seed 实现里的 run({ repository }) 改成 run(context) + context.repository(...)，同时绕开 @typescript-eslint/unbound-method。
影响 / 代价：约 15 分钟来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 04 · 实现阶段

新增 shadcn 原语后 pnpm 因 lockfile 过期直接失败

本任务已处理

ERR_PNPM_OUTDATED_LOCKFILE，pnpm install 被拒。

原因（实现者自述）
shadcn CLI 改了 package.json（把日期类包写进 dependencies），lockfile 未同步。
处理方式
pnpm install --no-frozen-lockfile，并按应用规则把 date-fns/react-day-picker 移到 devDependencies，保留 @nocobase/i18n 在 dependencies 及其 ^ 范围。
影响 / 代价：约 10 分钟来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 05 · 实现阶段

locale 键不能既是字符串又是对象，scheduler.enable 冲突

本任务已处理

启用按钮文案与启用成功 toast 都想用 scheduler.enable，类型校验冲突。

原因（实现者自述）
Locale 资源是映射类型，一个键只能有一个形状。
处理方式
toast 改名为 scheduler.enableSuccess / scheduler.disableSuccess，同时在 en-US 与 zh-CN 两侧补齐。
影响 / 代价：约 10 分钟来源：Agent · retro.json

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · Skill 与文档

nocobase-db Skill 的 seed 示例与本仓库 ESLint 规则冲突

需人工判断

SKILL.md 的 seed 示例写 async run({ repository }) 并调用 repository('orderStatuses')，但 @typescript-eslint/unbound-method 会对解构出来的 repository 报错；tests/logic/client-routes.test.ts 一类测试还会被 vitest/valid-title 拒绝 describe(常量) 的写法。照抄示例会直接自测失败。

建议改动

在 skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md 的 seed 片段里改用 run(context) + context.repository('name')，并在 nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md 补一句：describe/it 的标题要用字符串字面量，常量标识符会被 vitest/valid-title 拒绝。

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · Skill 与文档

首个 Scheduler/接口 E2E 的三件套缺少一份最小清单

需人工判断

createAppTest 必须传应用自己的 createServer 工厂、signIn 要传 TestApp 而不是 application、Cookie 写请求需要 auth.trustedOrigins + origin 头，这三点都只能从源码推断，各自都是一轮失败试错。

建议改动

在 nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md 增加“写第一个带登录的接口/E2E 测试”的最小清单（createServer、signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS)、auth.secret 与 trustedOrigins、写请求带 origin），并给出一个可直接复制的 createAppTest 片段。

来源：Agent · retro.json

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

为一个业务动作注册由 cron 触发的计划并看到真实执行结果直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
插件提供目标注册表、计划定义与触发/occurrence 记录；应用负责注册目标与计划
推荐使用方式
Provider.boot() 中 resolve schedulerServiceToken 后 registerTarget(...) + defineSchedule({key, schedule:{cron}, target})，并用 container.has() 守卫
Agent 实际使用
app/server/providers/todos.ts 的 boot() 注册 app.check-overdue-todos 目标与 '* * * * *' 计划
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E30 E1 E4 E5
在应用中管理计划（列表、启用停用、查看运行记录）正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
插件只提供 HTTP API 与 sync 命令，不提供页面；应用负责构建管理页
推荐使用方式
应用自行绘制计划/运行记录页并复用 scheduler.schedules 页面权限（operations.md 说明）
Agent 实际使用
app/client/routes.ts 定义 /scheduler 页并新增 client/pages/scheduler/*；验收 B01–B03 在此页完成
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/define.js:11–35

defineSchedule validates key regex, non-empty title, 5/6-field cron, timezone, target.type and limit/from-to, then normalizes the definition.

export function defineSchedule(definition) {
    if (!definition.key || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$/.test(definition.key))
        throw new Error('Schedule key must be a stable identifier.');
    if (!definition.title)
        throw new Error('Schedule title is required.');
    const parts = definition.schedule?.cron?.trim().split(/\s+/) ?? [];
    if (parts.length !== 5 && parts.length !== 6)
        throw new Error('Schedule cron must contain five or six fields.');
    const timezone = definition.schedule.timezone ?? 'UTC';
    try {
        CronExpressionParser.parse(definition.schedule.cron, {
            currentDate: new Date('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
            tz: timezone,
        });
    }
    catch {
        throw new Error('Schedule cron or timezone is invalid.');
    }
    if (!definition.target?.type)
        throw new Error('Schedule target type is required.');
    assertNoSensitiveConfig(definition.target.config);
    if (definition.schedule.limit !== undefined &&
        (!Number.isInteger(definition.schedule.limit) ||
            definition.schedule.limit < 1))
        throw new Error('Schedule limit must be a positive integer.');

原文件 SHA-256：00f0909276af5cc847ca3130860bb7fe917f8ea69b5e74d126610aebab10f51d

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/dispatch.js:22–33

Schedule dispatch derives occurrenceId from context.jobId, so one firing records and starts one occurrence (idempotent execution).

}
async function dispatch(spec, context, { targets, occurrences }) {
    // The occurrence is the firing: its id is the executor's job id, so the same
    // firing delivered twice records, and starts its target, once.
    const occurrenceId = context.jobId;
    const executionContext = { scheduleId: spec.id, occurrenceId };
    const record = {
        scheduleId: spec.id,
        definitionHash: spec.definitionHash,
        targetType: spec.target.type,
        scheduledAt: context.scheduledAt,
    };

原文件 SHA-256：2f586836bd5eba506dcc3a01a7bbcb86c184e779712d5ea6c12bf27bb9cb2495

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/tokens.js:1–8

schedulerServiceToken is the plugin's single public service; store, target registry and occurrence history stay private.

import { schedulerServiceToken as concreteSchedulerServiceToken } from './services/scheduler.js';
/**
 * The scheduler's one public service, carrying its one extension point.
 * Reading and changing schedules is reachable through the plugin's HTTP API
 * and its `scheduler sync` command; the store, the target registry and the
 * occurrence history stay private to this package.
 */
export const schedulerServiceToken=[REDACTED]

原文件 SHA-256：65c809ee20c3359c6eceb10f77270c725f328a011c51b7639ee0da62f18ebe35

E4 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:10–30

The Skill explains the execution model and that registerTarget is the only target extension surface.

Use the installed version's public exports, without importing plugin internals. The application owns business logic, schedule declarations, Providers, permissions, and deployment timing. Scheduler owns scheduling projections, trigger history, and execution state. The plugin's `skills/` directory is the source of truth; do not edit the synchronized application copy under `.agents/skills/`.

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                             |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce a heavier execution system                                       |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                               |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                   |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. A target does not guarantee exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency.

原文件 SHA-256：b068028d4f469d254eff36dd9aa38d62674285d25825a2f9c61937b762bb076d

E5 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/definitions.md:9–30

Reference shows imperative registration from a Provider's register()/boot() after resolving schedulerServiceToken, guarded by container.has().

There is no Server declaration field for schedules — nothing to add to `defineServerPlugin()`, and no conventional file path to discover. A schedule is registered imperatively, wherever the application or a plugin needs it, by resolving `schedulerServiceToken` and calling `defineSchedule(definition)`. Do this from a Provider's `register()` or `boot()`, guarded the same way a target registration is guarded, since the scheduler plugin might not be installed:

```ts
import { schedulerServiceToken } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/server/tokens';
import type { AppPluginApplication } from '@nocobase/app-server/plugins';
import { ServiceProvider } from '@nocobase/service-provider';

export default class MaintenanceScheduleProvider extends ServiceProvider<AppPluginApplication> {
  public readonly name = 'app/maintenance-schedule';

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.app.container.has(schedulerServiceToken)) return;
    this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken).defineSchedule({
      key: 'daily-maintenance',
      title: 'Daily maintenance',
      schedule: { cron: '0 2 * * *', timezone: 'Asia/Shanghai' },
      target: {
        type: 'app.maintenance',
        config: { batchSize: 100 },
      },
    });
  }

operations.md lists the management HTTP routes and states the plugin contributes no pages, so the application draws its own schedule list.

## Management Entry Points

The plugin contributes no pages: the application builds its schedule list and detail pages on the routes below and declares them among its own routes, guarded by the same permission. Definitions are maintained in source. The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions.

All routes below require authentication and authorization for `{ resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' }, action: 'access' }`, checked before the schedule is looked up. Enabling/disabling currently uses the same permission, not a separate update action.

| Method and path                                        | Response / effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules`                         | `{ data: ScheduleListItem[], meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`; `page` (default 1) and `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100)                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId`             | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences` | `{ data: ScheduleOccurrenceView[], meta: { nextPageToken? } }`, newest first; `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100) and `pageToken` from the previous page's `meta` |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable`     | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; enables the task, no request body                                                                                                    |

E10 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/context.js:15–30

createTaskRepositoryAccessor returns repository(collectionName) => unobservedRepository(connection, collectionName); task writes emit no Repository mutation events.

/**
 * Collection-level access bound to the connection the task runs on. Shared by
 * migrations and seeds so both reach the transaction's connection rather than
 * the application's DatabaseManager, which their service container withholds
 * for exactly that reason.
 *
 * Writes made here emit no Repository mutation events: a task runs while an
 * application installs or upgrades, before the services listeners depend on
 * are up, and its effect must not depend on which listeners happen to be
 * registered.
 */
export function createTaskRepositoryAccessor(connection) {
    return function repository(collectionName) {
        return unobservedRepository(connection, collectionName);
    };
}

E16 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/http-api.md:274–285

http-api.md requires every hand-written /api route to declare itself with describeRoute and states an agent learns endpoints from the API document, not route sources.

### Declaring a route

Every hand-written `/api` route declares itself with `describeRoute()` from `@nocobase/app-server/router`, as in the example above, after its authentication and permission middleware and before its `apiValidator()`s. A route that declares nothing is a defect the API check reports by name.

- **`tags`**: the plugin name in PascalCase, such as `Scheduler`, `Users` or `Agents`. The application's own routes tag the resource, such as `Orders`.
- **`summary`**: an English verb phrase, such as `Enable a Schedule`.
- **`operationId`**: the namespace, a verb and the resource in camelCase, unique across the application, such as `schedulerListOccurrences`; the application's own routes have no namespace, `cancelOrder`.
- **`description`**: what a caller cannot read from the schemas, such as the permission a route requires or which credential it expects. A credential that is not one of the document's security schemes is described here and only here.
- **Inputs**: `apiValidator()` for every path, query, header and JSON input, which documents the parameters and the body.
- **Responses**: `dataResponse(schema)` for `{ data }`, `listResponse(itemSchema, metaSchema?)` for `{ data, meta }`, `emptyResponse()` for a `204`, and the standard error body through `apiErrorResponse(status)` or `apiErrorResponse(409, 'When …')`. Do not list `400` for input validation: a route that uses `apiValidator` gets the `400` automatically. List `400` yourself only for another reason, such as a failed precondition. `apiErrorResponses` is `401`, `403` and `500`, for an authenticated route with a permission check; otherwise list each status the route can return with `apiErrorResponse(code)`.
- **Security**: a route a caller reaches without a credential, such as a status probe or the locales the sign-in page reads, declares `security: []`. Every other route inherits the document's top-level requirement, a session cookie (`cookieAuth`) or an API key in `x-api-key` (`apiKeyAuth`), which the authentication and API keys plugins contribute.
- **Schemas** live in the plugin's `server/routes/schemas.ts`. A schema several routes share carries `.meta({ ref: '<PluginName><Thing>' })`, such as `SchedulerSchedule`, and becomes a named component; public fields carry `.meta({ description })`, which stays next to the `$ref` when the field's schema is a shared one. A response object is documented open, so adding a field later is not a breaking change, unless its schema is `z.strictObject()`; a JSON body validated with `z.strictObject()` is documented closed. Recursive schemas such as `z.json()` need no workaround: they become components, named by their `ref` when they declare one.

原文件 SHA-256：9297b5c61d5a78e0d6481405117eca8ac8f9c7eb5dd61a8b0e2bbf5118cdc93c

E17 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:1–14

server-routes.md states endpoints live in server/routes/ and distinguishes the two kinds of routes.

# Server routes

HTTP endpoints live in `server/routes/` and are listed in the array `server/routes/index.ts` exports, which `server/runtime.ts` passes to the application.

## Two kinds

| Function             | Path in code     | Final URL        | For                                              |
| -------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `defineApiRoutes()`  | `/orders`        | `/api/orders`    | Application APIs the browser calls               |
| `defineRootRoutes()` | `/callbacks/pay` | `/callbacks/pay` | Webhooks, OAuth callbacks, protocol entry points |

Do not repeat `/api` in the path, and never write the deployment base path such as `/main` — both are added by the runtime.

Name paths, shape responses and errors, validate input and declare each route for the API document as [HTTP API design](http-api.md) describes. Every `/api` route carries a `describeRoute()` and validates its input with `apiValidator()`, both from `@nocobase/app-server/router`; a route without a declaration is a defect.

E22 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–49

signIn posts to the app's own sign-in route and TestSession.fetch only sets the cookie header; TEST_ORIGIN is defined but never applied to requests.

export async function signIn(app, credentials) {
    const apiRoot = `${TEST_ORIGIN}${trimTrailingSlash(app.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const byUsername = 'username' in credentials;
    const response = await app.fetch(new Request(`${apiRoot}/auth/sign-in/${byUsername ? 'username' : 'email'}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    }));
    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Sign-in as "${byUsername ? credentials.username : credentials.email}" failed with ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
    }
    const body = (await response.json());
    if (!body.user) {
        throw new Error('Sign-in succeeded without returning the signed-in user.');
    }
    const cookie = response.headers
        .getSetCookie()
        .map((header) => header.split(';')[0])
        .join('; ');
    return {
        user: body.user,
        cookie,
        fetch: async (path, init = {}) => {
            const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
            headers.set('cookie', [headers.get('cookie'), cookie].filter(Boolean).join('; '));
            return app.fetch(new Request(path.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${path}` : path, {
                ...init,
                headers,
            }));
        },
    };

E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:214–250

checkCookieWriteOrigin answers invalidCsrfOrigin for cookie-authenticated non-GET/HEAD/OPTIONS requests whose origin/referer is missing or untrusted.

    /** Protect writes authenticated by a browser cookie, including routes that skip normal session lookup. */
    async checkCookieWriteOrigin(context) {
        if (['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(context.req.method))
            return;
        // A credential header is not proof that it was used: an invalid API key may fall back to a valid cookie.
        // Cookie-free API key requests have no ambient browser credential to forge.
        if (!context.req.header('cookie'))
            return;
        const authContext = await this.auth.$context;
        const origin = context.req.header('origin');
        const inferredBaseURL = origin === 'null' &&
            context.req.header('sec-fetch-site') === 'same-origin'
            ? getBaseURL(undefined, authContext.options.basePath, context.req.raw, false, authContext.options.advanced?.trustedProxyHeaders)
            : undefined;
        const source = (inferredBaseURL ? getOrigin(inferredBaseURL) : undefined) ??
            origin ??
            context.req.header('referer');
        if (!source || source === 'null') {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
        // Better Auth also accepts per-request trusted origins. Its static context contains the
        // configured base URL, static origins, plugin origins, and BETTER_AUTH_TRUSTED_ORIGINS.
        const configuredOrigins = authContext.options.trustedOrigins;
        const mergedOrigins = typeof configuredOrigins === 'function'
            ? await configuredOrigins(context.req.raw)
            : (configuredOrigins ?? []);
        const requestAuthContext = Object.create(authContext);
        requestAuthContext.trustedOrigins = [
            ...authContext.trustedOrigins,
            ...mergedOrigins.filter((origin) => typeof origin === 'string' && Boolean(origin)),
        ];
        const trustedByAuth = requestAuthContext.isTrustedOrigin(source, {
            allowRelativePaths: false,
        });
        if (!trustedByAuth) {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }

E24 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–73

testing.md documents createAppTest({createServer: createStandaloneServer}) and signIn(...) but the example only issues a GET and never mentions Origin/trustedOrigins.

## Testing through the whole application

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';
import { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';
import { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';

const test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });

test('lists orders for an administrator only', async ({ testApp, request }) => {
  expect((await request('/orders')).status).toBe(401);
  const admin = await signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);
  expect((await admin.fetch('/orders')).status).toBe(200);
});
```

The application starts as `pnpm start` starts it and installs its migrations and seeds on start; one application serves the test file. A user other than the administrator is one a seed of the application created, signed in the same way.

E30 · app/server/providers/todos.ts:124–161

Applications register the target and schedule in Provider.boot() via schedulerServiceToken, guarded by container.has().

  public override async boot(): Promise<void> {
    // The Scheduler plugin is registered by the application; `has()` keeps this Provider usable without it.
    if (!this.app.container.has(schedulerServiceToken)) return;
    const scheduler = this.app.container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken);
    const todos = this.app.container.resolve(todosServiceToken);

    scheduler.registerTarget({
      type: CHECK_OVERDUE_TODOS_TARGET,
      title: 'Mark overdue todos as expired',
      validate(config) {
        return config !== null &&
          typeof config === 'object' &&
          !Array.isArray(config) &&
          Object.keys(config).length === 0
          ? { valid: true }
          : { valid: false, reason: 'config-must-be-empty-object' };
      },
      async start() {
        const expiredCount = await todos.expireOverdue();
        return {
          state: 'completed',
          outcome: 'succeeded',
          result: { expiredCount },
        };
      },
    });

    scheduler.defineSchedule({
      key: CHECK_OVERDUE_TODOS_SCHEDULE,
      title: 'Check overdue todos',
      description:
        'Marks every incomplete todo whose deadline has passed as expired.',
      // Every minute keeps the verification run short. A production application would use the business interval it
      // actually needs; the rule is idempotent either way.
      schedule: { cron: '* * * * *', timezone: 'UTC' },
      target: { type: CHECK_OVERDUE_TODOS_TARGET, config: {} },
    });
  }

E31 · app/database/main/seeds/202610100002_seed_todos.ts:25–58

The required seed uses defineSeed, run(context) and context.repository<TodoRow>('todos'), and is idempotent by title.

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202610100002_seed_todos',

  async run(context) {
    const todos = context.repository<TodoRow>('todos');
    const now = Date.now();
    // `dueAt` is an RFC 3339 instant: a deadline two days in the past, one a week out, and one already done.
    const rows = [
      {
        title: 'Submit the quarterly report',
        dueAt: new Date(now - 2 * DAY_MS).toISOString(),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: 'Prepare the release notes',
        dueAt: new Date(now + 7 * DAY_MS).toISOString(),
        completed: false,
        expired: false,
      },
      {
        title: 'Review the API documentation',
        dueAt: new Date(now + DAY_MS).toISOString(),
        completed: true,
        expired: false,
      },
    ];

    for (const row of rows) {
      const existing = await todos.findOne({ filter: { title: row.title } });
      if (existing) continue;
      await todos.createOne({ values: row });
    }
  },

export const todoApiRoutes: AppApiRouteContribution<Application> =
  defineApiRoutes((app) => {
    const router = new Hono();
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const todos = app.container.resolve(todosServiceToken);

    router.use('/todos', auth.required());
    router.use('/todos/*', auth.required());

    router.get(
      '/todos',
      describeRoute({
        tags: ['Todos'],
        summary: 'List todos',
        operationId: 'listTodos',
        responses: {
          '200': listResponse(Todo, TodoListMeta, 'Todos by deadline.'),
          '401': apiErrorResponse(401),
          '500': apiErrorResponse(500),
        },
      }),
      apiValidator('query', ListTodosQuery),

E33 · app/client/routes.ts:48–61

The application builds its own /scheduler page route with auth required and page grant scheduler.schedules, plus the /todos routes.

  {
    // The Scheduler plugin owns the schedules, their history and this page's permission, but contributes no page of
    // its own — so the application draws the list its administrators read. Stored page grants reference
    // `scheduler.schedules`, which is why the resource id is the plugin's and not a new one.
    auth: 'required',
    authz: {
      resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' },
      action: 'access',
    },
    componentLoader: () => import('./pages/scheduler/index.js'),
    name: 'scheduler',
    navigation: { title: 'navigation.scheduler', icon: CalendarClockIcon },
    path: '/scheduler',
  },

E66 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/cli.md:43–43

cli.md's origin guidance concerns the browser-facing deployment origin (app.publicOrigin, 403 INVALID_ORIGIN), not the cookie-write CSRF origin required by integration tests.

- **Verify the browser-facing origin before handing over a running application.** Set `app.publicOrigin` to the protocol, host and port the user actually opens, without the application's base path. On a remote development machine, inspect its LAN address or use the established domain instead of assuming `localhost`. An explicit `APP_PUBLIC_ORIGIN` overrides the file, including values in `.env` and `.env.local`; `pnpm dev` must not supply a fallback that overrides the configured origin. Verify an authentication request with the intended browser Origin as well as the page and health endpoint: those two GET requests alone cannot reveal a rejected login origin. `403 INVALID_ORIGIN` means the access address is not trusted; correct the origin configuration rather than disabling origin checks or trusting every host.

原文件 SHA-256：3665594985786c99887c0f14799c50bc36b19e7bda9b71c2dbad6746841a2ec2

E64 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/client/plugin.js:3–10

The scheduler plugin's client entry registers only locales through defineClientPlugin; its comment states the plugin contributes no pages and an application builds its own schedule page.

/**
 * Registers the plugin's locales, which hold the titles its server registers with the authorization catalog. It
 * contributes no pages: an application that lists schedules builds the page on the scheduler API among its own routes.
 */
const scheduler = defineClientPlugin({
    packageName: '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler',
    locales,
});

Business delivery context

业务交付与验收背景

已提交 PR · 等待人工评审

管理员通过 Scheduler 管理页观察到“检查过期待办”计划按每分钟 Cron 真实执行并留下执行记录：仅将已过期且未完成的待办标记为已过期，未来与已完成记录不受影响；重复执行不新增待办；停用/启用状态刷新后持久保存。

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

过期待办标记

截止已过且未完成的记录被计划标记为已过期，未来到期与已完成记录保持未过期。

真实调度执行与记录

Check overdue todos 按 * * * * * UTC 每分钟执行，Runs 记录 22:30 expiredCount:1、22:31 与 22:32 expiredCount:0。

重复执行幂等

同一目标多次执行，待办总数不变，已标记记录不被重复处理。

配置持久化

停用后刷新仍为 Disabled，重新启用后刷新为 Enabled。

原始验收要求

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

实际操作

  1. 以 nocobase 管理员登录 http://127.0.0.1:13000/main/ 进入业务界面
  2. 打开 Todos 页确认 3 条固定样例：过期未完成/未来已完成/未来未完成
  3. 打开 Schedules 页，确认存在计划 "Check overdue todos"（key app.check-overdue-todos），Cron "* * * * * UTC"，状态 Enabled
  4. 打开该计划 Actions -> View runs，查看执行记录
  5. 等待受控短周期（每分钟）自然触发下一次真实调度，再次查看 Runs 与 Todos

观察结果

  1. Schedules 列表显示 Check overdue todos / * * * * * UTC / Enabled / Next run Oct 15 2026 10:32:00 PM / Runs 2 runs · 2 succeeded
  2. Runs 弹窗显示两条真实执行记录：22:31:00 Succeeded result {"expiredCount":0}；22:30:00 Succeeded result {"expiredCount":1}
  3. Todos 列表："Submit the quarterly report" 截止 Oct 13 2026（已过期）未完成 -> Expired；"Review the API documentation" 截止 Oct 16 2026 已完成 -> Not expired；"Prepare the release notes" 截止 Oct 22 2026 未完成 -> Not expired
  4. 只有过期且未完成的记录被标记为 Expired，未来到期记录与已完成记录均保持 Not expired
  5. 页面无 Something went wrong / 空白页 / 未处理异常

来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

原始验收要求

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

实际操作

  1. 保持 Check overdue todos 计划启用，等待受控短周期（每分钟）再次自然触发同一目标
  2. 刷新 Schedules 页与 Runs 弹窗，对比多次执行记录
  3. 刷新 Todos 页，统计记录条数与各条状态

观察结果

  1. 同一目标 app.check-overdue-todos 重复执行：Runs 显示 3 runs · 3 succeeded（22:30:00、22:31:00、22:32:00）
  2. 三条执行记录结果依次为 {"expiredCount":1}、{"expiredCount":0}、{"expiredCount":0}，重复执行未再次处理已标记记录，也未抛错
  3. Todos 总数仍为 3 条（未新增重复待办）：Submit the quarterly report=过期未完成/Expired、Review the API documentation=已完成/Not expired、Prepare the release notes=未来未完成/Not expired
  4. 页面无 Something went wrong / 空白页 / 未处理异常

来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

03

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

本项仅验证配置持久化，未验证长期停用行为

A3 · 4 条操作 · 4 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

实际操作

  1. 在既有 Schedules 设置页，对 "Check overdue todos" 打开 Actions 菜单点击 Disable
  2. 观察到禁用成功提示 dialog：Disabled "Check overdue todos".，列表 Status 变为 Disabled，Next run 变为 —
  3. 完整刷新应用（重新打开 /main/scheduler），再次读取列表状态
  4. 随后重新启用并再次完整刷新，确认启用状态也可持久保存（结束时保持启用）

观察结果

  1. 点击 Disable 后列表即时显示 Status=Disabled、Next run=—，并弹出 "Disabled "Check overdue todos"." 提示
  2. 完整刷新 /main/scheduler 后仍显示 Status=Disabled、Next run=—，说明停用配置已真实持久保存
  3. 再点击 Enable 并完整刷新后显示 Status=Enabled、Next run=Oct 15 2026 10:33:00 PM，启用状态同样持久保存
  4. 本项仅验证配置持久化，未验证长期停用行为

来源：verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

B02. 再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

B03. 在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。

无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。
查看原始验收摘要与逐条数据

管理员登录后，在既有 Scheduler 管理页确认“Check overdue todos”(app.check-overdue-todos, * * * * * UTC) 计划启用，并观察到受控短周期内真实调度执行：Runs 记录 22:30 expiredCount:1、22:31/22:32 expiredCount:0；Todos 中只有过期且未完成的记录被标记 Expired，未来未完成与已完成记录保持 Not expired，重复执行待办总数仍为 3 条；停用后整页刷新仍为 Disabled，重新启用刷新后为 Enabled，配置持久保存。
